# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-19T12:33:44+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

- Wed, 18 Nov 2026 19:12:00 -0300 — Agência Brasil e Rádio Nacional ganham prêmio de direitos humanos
  A Agência Brasil e a Rádio Nacional , veículos da Empresa Brasil de Comunicação (EBC) , tiveram trabalhos reconhecidos pelo 43º Prêmio Direitos Humanos de Jornalismo, promovido pelo Movimento de Justiça e Direitos Humanos (MJDH), em parceria com a Ordem dos Advogados do Brasil do Rio Grande do Sul (OAB-RS). O prêmio estimula o trabalho dos profissionais de jornalismo na denúncia de violações e na defesa dos direitos humanos na América do Sul. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days. - Wed, 18 Nov 2026 19:11:15 +0000 — Are Uranus and Neptune Secretly "Rock Giants"? A Noble Gas Might Hold the Answer
  For decades, astronomers have divided the planets in the outer solar system into two distinct camps. Jupiter and Saturn were the “gas giants” made up primarily of hydrogen and helium. Uranus and Neptune were the “ice giants”, and were much smaller but were packed with heavier elements. A new paper from Olivier Mousis of the Southwest Research Institute and his co-authors, available in The Planetary Science Journal, explores whether we can use an isotope of a noble gas - argon-40 - to determine whether there are hidden mountains of rock under the ice giant’s thick atmospheres.

  Ministerio de la Mujer inicia jornada de prevención de la violencia en nueve demarcaciones priorizadas | Presidencia de la República Dominicana Pasar al contenido principal Compartir: Santo Domingo.- Ante la gravedad de los feminicidios registrados este año y el dolor que han dejado en las familias dominicanas, el Ministerio de la Mujer fortalece su estrategia de prevención , con una jornada nacional de orientación en nueve demarcaciones priorizadas por la incidencia de denuncias de violencia contra las mujeres y feminicidios. Los equipos del Ministerio llevarán información a barrios, centros educativos y espacios comunitarios para que más personas sepan reconocer señales de riesgo , acompañar a una mujer que pide ayuda y contactar a los servicios de atención y protección.   A Agência Brasil e a Rádio Nacional , veículos da Empresa Brasil de Comunicação (EBC) , tiveram trabalhos reconhecidos pelo 43º Prêmio Direitos Humanos de Jornalismo, promovido pelo Movimento de Justiça e Direitos Humanos (MJDH), em parceria com a Ordem dos Advogados do Brasil do Rio Grande do Sul (OAB-RS). #### Federal Register (government, en, feed)

- Thu, 19 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Airworthiness Directives; General Atomics AeroTec Systems GmbH (Type Certificate Previously Held by RUAG Aerospace Services GmbH) Airplanes
  The FAA is adopting a new airworthiness directive (AD) for all General Atomics AeroTec Systems GmbH (General Atomics) Model Dornier 228-100, Dornier 228-101, Dornier 228-200, Dornier 228-201, Dornier 228-202, and Dornier 228-212 airplanes. This AD was prompted by reports of specific part-numbered hydraulic pump motors becoming severely damaged by excessive heat. This AD requires replacing the affected hydraulic pump motor with an improved design hydraulic pump motor. This AD also prohibits installing an affected hydraulic pump motor on any airplane. The FAA is issuing this AD to address the unsafe condition on these products.
  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/19/2026-23633/airworthiness-directives-general-atomics-aerotec-systems-gmbh-type-certificate-previously-held-by

- undated — Sophia Space Tests Orbital-Compute Software on Kepler Constellation
  Sophia Space Tests Orbital-Compute Software on Kepler Constellation Skip to content Sophia Space has deployed its Sophia Orbital Operating System (SOOS) across multiple Kepler Communications optical-data-relay sats. The test, announced today, marks the first in-space milestone toward Sophia’s goal of scalable orbital-compute infrastructure, with SOOS as the base software layer for future architectures. As more data flows to and from—as well as between—satellites, operators are looking to process, manage, and deliver that data as efficiently as possible. Sophia is pitching SOOS as a way to do that across a distributed network in orbit. “We believe [the test] is the first time there will be a distributed network computer in orbit,” Sophia Space CEO and cofounder Rob DeMillo told Payload as the deployment was ongoing.&nbsp; Under the hood: SOOS is the software layer for Sophia’s future TILE
  https://payloadspace.com/sophia-space-tests-orbital-compute-software-on-kepler-constellation/
- undated — Exclusive: CUBECOM Expands to the Netherlands to Scale European Biz
  Exclusive: CUBECOM Expands to the Netherlands to Scale European Biz Skip to content Europe’s space industry is heating up, and it’s starting to attract outside attention. CUBECOM, the South African company developing RF communications systems for satellites, today announced it&#8217;s opening a new office in Delft, the Netherlands as part of an effort to lock down more deals across the continent. The new office will initially host the company’s sales and customer team, with the goal of forming stronger relationships with clients in the region, but will eventually include technical staff and engineers to develop European IP. CUBECOM ultimately aims to establish a regional production facility within the next five years.   https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-cubecom-expands-to-the-netherlands-to-scale-european-biz/

  https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-inflection-space-systems-books-first-orbital-mission/

- undated — Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth
  Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com NASA&#8217;s Voyager 1 spacecraft achieved another milestone this morning. Launched in 1977, it became the first human object to pass into interstellar space in August 2012 and is so far from Earth now that it takes one entire day for a radio signal to get there &#8212; one light-day. It also takes one light-day for Voyager 1 to send back the data it is still acquiring after all these decades. Voyager 1 passed the one light-day threshold at 5:16:07 am ET this morning at a distance of 16.094 billion miles (25.902 billion kilometers). That means it takes two days to communicate with Voyager &#8212; one day for the signal to get there and another day for the answer to come back. The spacecraft sent back stunning images of Jupiter in 1979 and Saturn in 1980. Since then it&#8217;s been on a quest to find w
  https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/voyager-1-reaches-new-milestone-one-light-day-from-earth/
- undated — NASA and FCC Nominations Advance Out of Committee Despite Democratic Boycott
  NASA and FCC Nominations Advance Out of Committee Despite Democratic Boycott &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com Nominations for two positions at NASA and one at the FCC were reported from the Senate Commerce Committee today even though no Democrats were in attendance. Committee rules state that a quorum is needed for official actions and that requires at least one Senator from the minority party, but they were approved nonetheless. President Trump nominated Joshua Shoemaker to be NASA&#8217;s Chief Financial Officer and Robert Steinau to be Inspector General, as well as Danielle Thumann Severs to be an FCC commissioner. The committee held a hearing on their nominations along with a nominee for the Department of Transportation on November 10. FCC nominee Danielle Thumann Severs at her nomination hearing before the Senate Commerce Committee, November 10, 2026. Screenshot. The Shoemaker and Stei
  https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/nasa-and-fcc-nominations-advance-out-of-committee-despite-democratic-boycott/
- undated — Insufficient Fuel Flow May Have Doomed Vulcan
  Insufficient Fuel Flow May Have Doomed Vulcan &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com The United Launch Alliance said today that insufficient fuel flow from the launch vehicle to the BE-4 engines may have led to the failure of Vulcan Leo 1 on Sunday. The fifth flight of ULA&#8217;s new Vulcan rocket failed about 10 minutes after liftoff on November 15, the company&#8217;s first total failure in 20 years of operation. By yesterday they had traced the problem to &#8220;low booster performance,&#8221; referring to the rocket&#8217;s first stage. The first stage is powered by two BE-4 liquid methane/liquid oxygen (methalox) engines built by Blue Origin, which uses them for its own New Glenn rocket. In an update today, ULA says preliminary indications are that it was not the engines themselves, but &#8220;inadequate fuel supply from the vehicle&#8221; to the engines.   Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. McGuinty, Minister of National Defence, announced an historic $200 million investment in core infrastructure for a Canadian-owned spaceport, along with other major new space-related capabilities and initiatives. Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content An artist’s rendering of Reaction Dynamics’ Aurora, left, and the larger Borealis. Credit: Reaction Dynamics. Credit: Reaction Dynamics NATO said Tuesday its STARLIFT space initiative is under way, with Canada among 10 Allies and non-NATO member South Korea paying to reserve space on commercial rockets for military satellite launches. The countries signed their funding commitments Oct. 29 on the margins of a meeting of NATO&#8217;s national armaments directors, and NATO announced them at the ongoing EDGE 26 conference in Izmir, Türkiye . Belgium, Finland, Germany, Italy, the Netherlands, Norway, Sweden, Türkiye and the United Kingdom are the other Allies financing the work, through the NATO Support and Procurement Agency (NSPA). - undated — Vyoma to fly space-tracking sensors on 10 Kepler satellites
  Vyoma to fly space-tracking sensors on 10 Kepler satellites - SpaceQ Media Inc. Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content AETHER optical data relay network satellites testing. Credit: Kepler Communications Munich-based Vyoma said Tuesday it has signed an agreement with Kepler Communications to fly its space-tracking sensors on 10 of the Toronto company’s next-generation relay satellites, which are due to launch no earlier than October 2028. The sensors will ride on Kepler Communications’ Tranche 2 satellites, the next generation of an optical data relay network that moves data between spacecraft and down to the ground by laser. Kepler launched its first 10 relay satellites in January and started commercial service in August. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

- undated — Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare
  Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare &#8211; MEDIA INAF Un drone sorvola un lago prosciugato nel deserto della California, eseguendo una serie di manovre che ricordano la discesa di un veicolo spaziale verso la superficie lunare. Non è una coincidenza: il volo fa parte di un esperimento della Nasa per rendere più sicuri e precisi i futuri allunaggi. Ma il drone sulla Luna non ci andrà mai: senza atmosfera, le sue eliche non potrebbero farlo volare. A interessare la Nasa è il sistema che trasporta, una tecnologia progettata per riconoscere i pericoli durante la discesa e aiutare i veicoli spaziali a scegliere autonomamente dove atterrare. Si chiama Splice , acronimo di Safe and Precise Landing &#8211; Integrated Capabilities Evolution , ed è un insieme di tecnologie di guida, navigazione e controllo sviluppate dall’agenzia spaziale statunitense per le future missioni sulla Luna,
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/10/un-drone-nel-deserto-per-imparare-ad-atterrare-sulla-luna/
- undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

  ## [Een mislukte lancering, een geslaagde landing en heel veel ruimtetelescopen Verder de mijlpaal van één lichtdag van de Voyager 1, een mogelijke nieuwe dwergplaneet - nog zonder naam - en een Chinese missie naar de Jupitermaan Callisto. Dat en meer bespreken Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout en Herbert Blankesteijn in deze nieuwe Space Cowboys. Schrijf je in voor de nieuwsbrief van Space Cowbows en krijg elke aflevering in je mailbox zodra deze uitkomt - op thysroes.nl/spacecowboys SpaceCowboys behandelt ruimtevaart- en astronomienieuws van land, planeet en daarbuiten. Afwisselend gepresenteerd door: Thijs Roes, Herbert Blankesteijn, Michel van Baal, Luc van den Abeelen, Philippe Schoonejans, Erik Laan, Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout, Inge Loes ten Kate, André Kuipers, Bastiaan Bom, Nadine Duursma, Marco Langbroek, Bruno van Wayenburg, Charlotte Pouwels, Marc Heemskerk en Nick Poelstra.   Home - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Het ruimtevaartagentschap van de rijksoverheid Meer over NLSA Ga snel naar Innovatie support Satelliet dataportaal GEO Netherlands NL Space Onderwijs en educatie NLSA Advies Nieuws Project 19 november 2026 Vier projecten geselecteerd voor ruimtevaart innovatiechallenge veiligheidsdomein De ruimte-infrastructuur is van vitaal belang voor de veiligheid van Nederland en Europa. Met de Innovatie Impact Challenge (ICC) Ruimtevaart voor het veiligheidsdomein investeert Nederland in vier innovatieve technologieën die een veilig en duurzaam gebruik van de ruimte bevorderen. Satellieten zorgen voor navigatie, weerinformatie, wereldwijde communicatie en ze ondersteunen militairen tijdens defensieoperaties. - undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 18 november 2026 20 jaar ESERO Onderwijs: ruimtevaart als vonk voor toekomstig talent Lees meer
  20 jaar ESERO Onderwijs: ruimtevaart als vonk voor toekomstig talent - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zelf een minisatelliet bouwen, trainen als een astronaut of de satelliet van de toekomst ontwerpen. Lessen en activiteiten van ESERO Onderwijs brengen al twintig jaar ruimtevaart in de klas. Gisteren vierde het programma zijn jubileum bij ESTEC in Noordwijk. ESERO onderwijs begon met een pilot in 2006. Na een succesvol scholenexperiment tijdens de eerste ruimtemissie van André Kuipers – bijna 80.000 leerlingen deden mee aan seeds in space – sloegen ESA en de Nederlandse overheid de handen ineen om werk te maken van ruimtevaarteducatie. Ruimtevaart kan scholieren inspireren en op het pad zetten van een carrière in wetenschap of techniek, was ook de overtuiging van het Ministerie van Onderwijs, Cultuur en Wetenschap. &nbsp; Twintig jaar later is ESERO onderwijs uitgegroeid

- undated — Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick
  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

#### State Secretariat for Education, Research and Innovation (SBFI/SERI), Swiss Space Office (space_office, de, newsroom)

- undated — Staatssekretariat für Bildung, Forschung und Innovation SBFI Staatssekretariat für Bildung, Forschung und Innovation SBFI
  Das Staatssekretariat für Bildung, Forschung und Innovation Das Staatssekretariat für Bildung, Forschung und Innovation Das SBFI ist das Kompetenzzentrum des Bundes für national und international ausgerichtete Fragen der Bildungs-, Forschungs- und Innovationspolitik. Gemeinsam mit unseren Partnern setzen wir uns dafür ein, dass die Schweiz auch in Zukunft über einen leistungsfähigen BFI-Bereich verfügt.   Le ravitaillement en orbite se prépare à Kockelscheuer | Paperjam News The Exploration Company et Absolut System ont inauguré leurs bureaux à Kockelscheuer le 16 novembre. (Photo: photographer.lu) The Exploration Company et Absolut System ont ouvert des bureaux à Kockelscheuer. Leur projet commun vise à stocker et transférer du carburant dans l’espace, une technologie qui pourrait prolonger les missions des engins spatiaux. Faire le plein une fois dans l’espace: c’est le projet que The Exploration Company et Absolut System comptent faire avancer depuis le Luxembourg. Les deux entreprises ont inauguré leurs bureaux à Kockelscheuer ce lundi 16 novembre. Elles y développeront Cryokit, un système destiné au stockage et au transfert de carburants cryogéniques en orbite. Le premier prototype sera réalisé au Luxembourg avec de l’azote liquide. This exercise resulted in the first edition of the Catalogue of Analogue Space Sites and Infrastructures in Portugal, available here. Intending to update the Catalogue in the future, the Portuguese Space Agency invites you to complete the survey below, which will be continuously open and available for submission.   Monaco à la conquête de l&#039;espace --> Sections --> Découvrir Monaco Célébrités Culture Évènements Informations Locales Monaco Dans Le Monde Sortir Structures Sportives Tourisme --> Rejoignez nos 150 000 followers ! Publicité » --> La création du Bureau des affaires spatiales monégasques vient renforcer l&rsquo;attrait de la Principauté pour ce secteur. Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. L’enjeu est triple : accompagner les entreprises du domaine spatial déjà implantées sur le territoire monégasque, créer une base attractive pour celles qui veulent s’installer en Principauté et représenter Monaco à l’inte

Na náhledovém snímku dnešního článku vidíte Didymos, větší ze dvou planetek, které dominuje dramatický rovníkový hřeben. Fotka byla pořízena ze vzdálenosti 28,5 kilometru, ale není to jediný prostředek, jak může sonda pozorovat. Hera disponuje kombinací viditelného, multispektrálního a&nbsp;tepelně-infračerveného pozorování. Tento tahoun v&nbsp;oboru pozorování Země byl přitom navržen jen na šestiletou službu. Poté, co pracovala ještě více než 4× déle, se již na družici projevuje stárnutí systémů a&nbsp;také klesá její oběžná dráha. Více než čtvrt století Terra poskytovala detailní záznamy o&nbsp;změnách využívání pevniny, chování lesních požárů, přenosu prachu a&nbsp;kouře, atmosférickém složení i&nbsp;ledovém příkrovu. Mise COSI má hledět do kosmického prostoru a&nbsp;pozorovat jej s&nbsp;širokým zorným polem v&nbsp;oblasti gama záření, což je nejenergetičtější forma elektromagnetického záření. Mezi její vědecké úkoly patří studium energetických jevů, které probíhají v&nbsp;Mléčné dráze i&nbsp;mimo ni, jako jsou vznik a&nbsp;zánik hmoty a&nbsp;antihmoty či závěrečné fáze života hvězd. - undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. W przypadku lądowania w Kalifornii przewidywane są krótkotrwałe fale uderzeniowe powstające podczas przekraczania bariery dźwięku. Według FAA ich natężenie nie powinno przekraczać poziomu porównywalnego z odgłosem grzmotu. ULA podkreśla, że problem nie wynikał z wydajności samych silników BE-4, lecz z ograniczonego dopływu paliwa z systemu rakiety. Awaria rakiety Vulcan. 40 satelitów Amazon Leo utraconych Jak poinformowała firma, silniki RL10 drugiego stopnia Centaur V pracowały zgodnie z założeniami . Również sześć boosterów bocznych na paliwo stałe (SRB) osiągnęło nominalne parametry pracy. Oznacza to, że wcześniejsze problemy związane z dyszami silników pomocniczych, które wystąpiły podczas poprzednich lotów Vulcana, nie powtórzyły się podczas ostatniej misji.   Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Til hovedinnhold Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Norge har bygget opp en rominfrastruktur både til kommersiell og offentlig bruk. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid. Nasjonale satellitter AISSat-1 (2010-2022) AISSat-1 var en pionér i Norge og i verden. Eier Direktoratet for romvirksomhet Hovedoppgave Skipsovervåking Dimensjoner 20x20x20 cm. 6 kg.   Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Dr. Farhan: The Kuwait National Space Research Center is a national platform for excellence in space research. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno

  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos.   ![Image 1](https://crts.gov.ma/img/logo.png) ###### PROJET SCO IRRISAT ## GESTION OPTIMALE DE L'EAU DESTINEE A L'IRRIGATION Le projet SCO IRRISAT, une initiative novatrice qui vise à répondre aux défis liés à la gestion optimale de l'eau agricole au Maroc, en particulier dans les régions semi-arides. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre. [Voir plus](https://crts.gov.ma/sco_irrisat.html) ###### Image Satellite ## Port Casablanca Maroc Exploration fascinante du port de Casablanca, figé dans le temps. Cette vue aérienne offre un aperçu spectaculaire de l'activité portuaire, témoignant de l'importance stratégique de cette plaque tournante maritime. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma/#) ###### Image Satellite ## Station Noor Ouarzazate Plongez dans l'éclat captivant de la Station Noor à Ouarzazate. Une merveille d'ingénierie solaire, cette image révèle la splendeur technologique et la contribution majeure de cette station à la transition énergétique, capturant l'essence même de l'innovation marocaine. - undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection.   About ZINGSA &#8212; Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency Phone +263 8677009885 Email publicrelations@zingsa.ac.zw Hours Mon&#8211;Fri, 8am&#8211;4pm Contact Us About The ZINGSA story Promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial technologies for Zimbabwe&#8217;s development. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization.   https://zingsa.ac.zw/about.html

ZIMSAT-1 Zimbabwe&#8217;s first satellite, successfully launched in November 2022. Used for monitoring drought conditions, mapping mining activities, assessing crop health, and generating accurate weather forecasts. ZIMSAT-2 Zimbabwe&#8217;s second satellite mission, building on the success of ZIMSAT-1 and expanding national capabilities in space technology and Earth observation. CubeSat Development Advanced CubeSat development using 3D printing technology and modern engineering practices to build indigenous small-satellite capability. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India.   Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Home / Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully November 12, 2026 The ground static test of the PSLV third stage (PS3) was carried out successfully at the Static Test Facility in Satish Dhawan Space Centre, Sriharikota on November 12, 2026. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far.   โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา วิสัยทัศน์อนาคตด้านอวกาศ ครอบคลุมทั้งการปกป้องโลกให้ดีขึ้น การนำประโยชน์จากอวกาศมาใช้งานในโลก รวมทั้งการแสวงหาความเป็นไปได้อื่นๆ ที่มนุษย์ยังไปไม่ถึง ทิศทางอนาคต จากผู้นำเอกชนระดับโลกด้านอวกาศอย่าง &ldquo; อีลอน มัสก์ &rdquo; ผู้ก่อตั้ง บริษัทสเปซเอ็กซ์ (SpaceX) วางวิสัยทัศน์ไปสู่การตั้งถิ่นฐานของมนุษย์โลกที่ดาวอังคาร ในขณะที่ผู้นำภาคธุรกิจอย่าง &ldquo; เจฟฟ์ เบซอส &rdquo; จาก บริษัทบลู ออริจิน (Blue Origin) มีแผนอนาคตที่จะทำให้ดวงจันทร์เป็นอาณานิคมแห่งใหม่ของโลก เพื่อตอบโจทย์ข้อท้าทายที่โลกไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้ จากวิสัยทัศน์อนาคตของผู้นำระดับโลกเหล่านี้ เป็นกุญแจสำคัญในการต่อยอดโอกาสของ เศรษฐกิจอวกาศที่นานาประเทศจับตามอง 5 แนวทาง ได้แก่ 1. การเดินทางสู่อวกาศ (Going to space)

  Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. - undated — Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’
  Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ In Focus Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ MONDAY, SEPTEMBER 05, 2022 | THE NATION สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added. Haji Azlikamil Napiah merasmikan Sambutan Hari Keluarga Peringkat MYSA 2026 bertempat di Dewan Seminar, Ibu Pejabat MYSA pada 16 November 2026. Sambutan ini bertepatan pemilihan November sebagai Bulan Keluarga Kebangsaan sejak tahun 2024. . Program merupakan anjuran bersama PUSPANITA MYSA dan Kelab MYSAC dengan tema sambutan “KASIH Keluarga, Jiwa Negara” telah disertai lebih 100 warga kerja. Secara tidak langsung, program mengeratkan hubungan di antara majikan dan pekerja selain meningkatkan amalan nilai murni melalui aktiviti sambutan hari keluarga.
